Bill Tracker · Delaware

Every bill in the 153rd General Assembly

Updated October 14, 2026

Every bill, substitute and resolution introduced in the Delaware General Assembly from 2025 to 2026, with its status and last action in the legislature's own words.

How to read this. This is every bill, substitute and resolution the Delaware General Assembly lists for its 153rd General Assembly, with the same fields for each one. Titles, sponsors, statuses and last actions are the legislature's own words. A status that names a committee, such as Judiciary, is the committee the legislature lists for that bill. Abbreviations are the legislature's; see its glossary of terms. We do not pick, rank or group bills by topic. Rows are in the legislature's own numbering order, and you can sort them by date instead. Amendments are not listed as rows: the bill number shows the amendments the legislature has attached (for example "w/ HA 1"), and each bill's official page lists them all. The legislature's site, legis.delaware.gov, is the authority. If this page and the legislature differ, the legislature is right and we fix this page.

All legislation, 153rd General Assembly

1,484 items: 831 bills, 187 substitutes, 53 simple resolutions, 378 concurrent resolutions and 35 joint resolutions.
